local function clock(a1) -- Line: 110; types: a1: number
    local v1 = math.max(0, (math.ceil(a1)))

    if v1 >= 3600 then
        return (("%*h %*m"):format(v1 // 3600, v1 % 3600 // 60))
    end

    if v1 >= 60 then
        return (("%*m %*s"):format(v1 // 60, v1 % 60))
    end

    return (("%*s"):format(v1))
end

local function tipFor(a1, a2) -- Line: 121; upvalues: Config (val), Grid (val); types: a1: string, a2: table

    if a1 == "Chicken" then
        if not a2.grown then
            return "Still a chick. It starts laying once it grows up.", false
        end

        if not a2.nearHouse then
            return (("Chickens only lay in a Hen House. Place one within %* blocks."):format((math.floor(Farm.HouseReachStuds / Grid.CellSize)))),
                false
        end

        if not a2.housed then
            return (("That Hen House is full (%* chickens). Place another nearby."):format(Farm.HenHouseOccupants)),
                false
        end

        if not a2.nearFood then
            return (("Place a Chicken Feeder within %* blocks."):format((math.floor(Farm.FeederReachStuds / Grid.CellSize)))),
                false
        end

        if not a2.fed then
            return "Its feeder is empty. Fill it with seeds.", false
        end

        return "Laying eggs in its Hen House.", true
    end

    if a1 == "Cow" then
        if not a2.nearHay then
            return (("Place a Hay Feeder within %* blocks."):format((math.floor(Config.Animal.Milk.HayReachStuds / Grid.CellSize)))),
                false
        end

        if not a2.fed then
            return "Its Hay Feeder is empty. Fill it with Hay Bales.", false
        end

        return "Eating hay and making milk.", true
    end

    if a1 ~= "Sheep" then
        return "", true
    end

    if not a2.grass then
        return "No grass to graze nearby. Eaten grass grows back over time, and all at once in the rain.", false
    end

    return "Grazing and growing wool.", true
end

local function drawResult(a1, a2, a3)
    -- Line: 645
    -- upvalues: u109 (ref), Ui (val), specFor (val), ArcaneCombine (val), fillSquare (val), QualityColors (val)
    -- upvalues: statText (val)
    -- types: a1: table?, a2: table?, a3: table?
    local v1 = u109

    if a1 and a2 and a3 then
        local v2, v3, v4
        local v5 = specFor(a1.itemId)

        if not ArcaneCombine.canImprove(v5, a2, a3) then
            local square = v1.square

            for i, j in square.holder:GetChildren() do
                j:Destroy()
            end

            square.name.Text = ""
            square.tag.Text = ""

            Ui.restyle(square.frame, "Slot")

            v1.shown = nil
            v1.quality.Text = "No Better"
            v1.quality.TextColor3 = Ui.Tone.Bad
            v1.stats.Text = ""

            return false
        end

        if a2.outcome ~= a3.outcome then
            v3 = ArcaneCombine.score(v5, a2)
            v2 = if not (ArcaneCombine.score(v5, a3) <= v3) then a3 else a2
            v3, v4 = ArcaneCombine.promotion(v5, a2, a3)

            if not (v3 > 0) or not v4 then
                fillSquare(
                    v1.square,
                    a1.itemId,
                    {n = 0, outcome = v2.outcome, stats = v2.stats}
                )

                v1.shown = v2
                v1.quality.Text = ("%* Or Better"):format(v2.outcome)
                local quality_6 = v1.quality
                local outcome_3 = v2.outcome
                quality_6.TextColor3 = if outcome_3 == nil then
                    Ui.Tone.Info
                else
                    if outcome_3 ~= "Standard" then
                        Color3.fromHex(QualityColors[outcome_3] or QualityColors.Standard)
                    else
                        Ui.Tone.Info
                v1.stats.Text = ("At least %*"):format((statText(a1.itemId, v2)))
            else
                fillSquare(
                    v1.square,
                    a1.itemId,
                    {n = 0, outcome = v4, stats = v2.stats}
                )

                v1.shown = v2
                v1.quality.Text = ("%*%% %*"):format(math.round(v3 * 100), v4)
                v1.quality.TextColor3 = if v4 == nil then
                    Ui.Tone.Info
                else
                    if v4 ~= "Standard" then
                        Color3.fromHex(QualityColors[v4] or QualityColors.Standard)
                    else
                        Ui.Tone.Info
                v1.stats.Text = ("Otherwise a Perfect %*"):format(v2.outcome)
            end
        else
            v2, v3, v4 = ArcaneCombine.result(
                v5,
                a2,
                a3,
                function() -- Line: 665
                    return 0
                end
            )
            local v6 = {n = 0, outcome = v2, stats = v3, perfect = v4}

            fillSquare(v1.square, a1.itemId, v6)

            v1.shown = v6
            local v7 = if not v4 then "" else "Perfect "
            v1.quality.Text = ("%*%*"):format(v7, v2)
            local quality_2 = v1.quality
            local Info = if v2 == nil then
                Ui.Tone.Info
            else
                if v2 ~= "Standard" then
                    Color3.fromHex(QualityColors[v2] or QualityColors.Standard)
                else
                    Ui.Tone.Info
            quality_2.TextColor3 = Info
            v1.stats.Text = statText(a1.itemId, v6)
        end

        return true
    end

    local square_4 = v1.square

    for k, n in square_4.holder:GetChildren() do
        n:Destroy()
    end

    square_4.name.Text = ""
    square_4.tag.Text = ""

    Ui.restyle(square_4.frame, "Slot")

    v1.shown = nil
    v1.quality.Text = "Result"
    v1.quality.TextColor3 = Ui.Palette.Dim
    v1.stats.Text = ""

    return false
end

local function dropTargetAt(a1)

    -- upvalues: LocalPlayer (val), GuiService (val), u268 (val), u258 (ref)
    -- types: a1: userdata

end

    -- upvalues: Bees (val), wanderOn (val), nextBloom (val), flyTo (val)

    end

            + Vector3.new(0, math.sin((a2 + a1.phase) * 4) * 0.08, 0)

            a1.state = "fly"

        end

    -- upvalues: Bees (val)
    -- types: a1: table, a2: number, a3: table, a4: table

local function shortTime(a1) -- Line: 294; types: a1: number
    local v1, v2, v3

    if a1 < 10 then
        v1 = math.floor(a1 * 10 + 0.5) / 10

        if v1 == math.floor(v1) then
            return (("%*s"):format((math.floor(v1))))
        end

        return (("%*s"):format(v1))
    end

    if a1 < 60 then
        return (("%*s"):format((math.floor(a1 + 0.5))))
    end

    if a1 < 3600 then
        v1 = math.floor(a1 + 0.5)
        v2 = v1 // 60
        v3 = v1 % 60

        if v3 == 0 then
            return (("%*m"):format(v2))
        end

        return (("%*m %*s"):format(v2, v3))
    end

    v1 = math.floor(a1 / 60 + 0.5)
    v2 = v1 // 60
    v3 = v1 % 60

    if v3 == 0 then
        return (("%*h"):format(v2))
    end

    return (("%*h %*m"):format(v2, v3))
end

        v1,

local function closeIfOutOfRange()
    -- Line: 1611
    -- upvalues: u205 (ref), u151 (ref), u53 (val), LocalPlayer (val), Config (val)
    local v1 = u205

    if v1 and u151.Visible then
        if not v1.Parent then
            u53.close()
            return
        end

        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not HumanoidRootPart then
            return
        end

        local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1.Position).Magnitude

        if Config.Player.InteractReachStuds + 8 < Magnitude then
            u53.close()
        end
    end
end

local function recipeLines(a1, a2)
    -- Line: 1633
    -- upvalues: Config (val), u149 (ref), LocalPlayer (val), Palette (val)
    -- types: a1: string, a2: string
    local v1, v2, v3, v4, v5
    local v6 = Config.Description.BeltTips[a1]

    if v6 then
        return v6
    end

    v6 = {}
    local v7 = nil
    local v8 = nil

    for i, j in Config.Recipe.All, v7, v8 do
        if table.find(j.stations, a2)
            and j.family == nil
            and j.workbenchTier <= u149
            and not Config.Recipe.isSecret(j.output)
            and Config.Release.mayCraft(LocalPlayer, j.output) then
            v4 = {}

            for k, n in j.inputs do
                table.insert(
                    v4,
                    {name = Palette.displayName(k), count = n}
                )
            end

            table.sort(
                v4,
                function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1651
                    if a1.count ~= a2.count then
                        return a2.count < a1.count
                    end

                    return a1.name < a2.name
                end
            )

            v5 = {}
            v2 = nil
            v3 = nil

            for m, i5 in v4, v2, v3 do
                table.insert(v5, if not (1 < i5.count) then i5.name else ("x%* %*"):format(i5.count, i5.name))
            end

            v1 = Palette.displayName(j.output)
            v2 = if not (1 < j.outputCount) then v1 else ("x%* %*"):format(j.outputCount, v1)

            table.insert(
                v6,
                {
                    key = ("%* %*"):format(if not v4[1] then "" else v4[1].name, v1),
                    text = ("%* = %*"):format(table.concat(v5, " + "), v2),
                }
            )
        end
    end

    table.sort(
        v6,
        function(a1, a2) -- Line: 1668
            return a1.key < a2.key
        end
    )

    local v9 = {}

    for i6, i7 in v6 do
        table.insert(v9, i7.text)
    end

    return v9
end

            end

    end

local function step(a1)
    -- Line: 362
    -- upvalues: u72 (val), fidget (val), Customer (val), standAt (val), play (val)
    -- types: a1: number
    local Run, Unit, onArrive, playing, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5
    local v6 = nil
    local v7 = nil

    for i, j in u72, v6, v7 do
        if not j.busy and j.model.Parent then
            v2 = j.path[1]

            if v2 then
                v3 = j.root.Position - Vector3.new(0, j.lift, 0)
                v4 = Vector3.new(v2.X - v3.X, v2.Y - v3.Y, v2.Z - v3.Z)
                v5 = Customer.RunSpeed * j.pace * a1

                if not (v4.Magnitude <= v5) then
                    if j.playing ~= "Run" then
                        playing = j.playing and j.tracks[j.playing]

                        if playing then
                            playing:Stop(0.15)
                        end

                        j.playing = "Run"
                        Run = j.tracks.Run

                        if Run then
                            Run:Play(0.15)
                        end
                    end

                    v1 = v3 + v4.Unit * v5
                    standAt(j, v1, v1 + v4)
                else
                    Unit = if not (0.001 < v4.Magnitude) then
                        Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    else
                        v4.Unit
                    standAt(j, v2, v2 + Unit)
                    table.remove(j.path, 1)

                    if #j.path == 0 then
                        play(j, "Idle")

                        if j.faceToward and j.place then
                            standAt(j, v2, j.faceToward)
                        end

                        onArrive = j.onArrive
                        j.onArrive = nil

                        if onArrive then
                            onArrive()
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif j.settled and j.place then
                fidget(j)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function plural(a1, a2) -- Line: 494; upvalues: Palette (val), u98 (val); types: a1: string, a2: number

    if a2 == 1 then
        return v1
    end

        if string.sub(v1, -#j) == j then
            return v1
        end
    end

        return v1
    end

    return (("%*s"):format(v1))
end
